[\[240a.1\]]rgyal ba'i sku la mngon phyogs par/_/bsam gtan ngoms phyir rtag tu gnas/_/zhes gsungs so/_/dkyil 'khor thams cad du sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni tshogs yongs su rdzogs pas pad+ma dang zla ba'am/_nyi ma la bzhugs par bya la/_
[\[240a.2\]]'phags pa nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas rnams ni ye shes dang tshogs phyogs gcig pa yin pa'i phyir/_de dag ni pad+ma 'dab ma ya re zhig la 'khod par bya zhing tshangs pa la sogs pa 'jig rten pa'i lha de dag ni/_tshogs ma lus par bsags par 'dod cing /_khams gsum la chags 
[\[240a.3\]]pa dang bral ba'i phyir pad+ma dmar po la 'khod par bya la/_'jig rten pa phal pa rnams ni yon tan de dag dang yang mi ldan pas rang gi stan smos pa med na/_sa nyid la 'khod par bya ste/_ji skad du rnam par snang mdzad mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa las gsungs pa/_sangs rgyas rnams 
[\[240a.4\]]ni thams cad dang /_/de sras blo can rnams kyi gdan/_/pad+ma yid las 'byung ba'i mchog_/bkra shis 'jig rten rnams kyis bsngags/_/rang sangs rgyas dang dgra bcom pa/_/phyogs gcig shes rab gang grags pa/_/de dag stan du shes par bya/_/pad+ma las byung 'dab ma 
[\[240a.5\]]pa'o/_/'jig rten gyi ni lha rnams gang /_/tshangs sogs pa yi 'jig rten grags/_/de dag stan ni pad+ma dmar/_/rgyal po rnams kyis bsngags pa'o/_/phal pa rnams ni ci rigs par/_/sa yi bye brag dag la bsam/_/zhes so/_/de bzhin du gang du kha dog dang dbyibs dang /_
[\[240a.6\]]tshad nges par ma gsungs pa der/_rang gi yid la ji ltar dga' bar bsgom par bya'i 'di lung gsal po med do zhes yid gnyis mi bya ste/_de ltar yang rnam par snang mdzad mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa de nyid las/_gzugs ni gnyis su shes par bya/_/bud med dag ni skyes 
[\[240a.7\]]pa nyid/_/yang na kun du thams cad kyi/_/kha dog dbyibs dang gzugs rnams te/_/bsam pas ye shes las byung bas/_/de bas de rnams bsam yas te/_/ye shes mkhyen pa'i ye shes ni/_/sems can don phyir tha dad gyur/_/yid kyi rgya ni ji tsam par/_/stan dang phyag rgya 
[\[240b\]]
[\[240b.1\]]dag dang ni/_/lha yi tshad kyang [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin/_/de tsam du ni shes par bya/_/zhes gsungs so/_/rim pa bzhi po de nyid la sngags pa de'i snying rje rab tu bskyed pa dang bcom ldan 'das khams gsum rnam par rgyal bas ngan 'gro bkug par ston pa'i bdag nyid/_shar du sems can dmyal 
[\[240b.2\]]ba rnams dang /_lhor dud 'gro rnams dang /_nub tu yi dwags rnams dang /_byang du phug ron dkar po dang /_sbrul nag po dang /_rtsangs pa nag po dang /_phag rnams las phag gis gsum po za ba ltar gnas par shes par bya'o/_/de ltar rtsa ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi lha rnams bstan 
[\[240b.3\]]nas/_da ni rdo rje gtsug tor gyi lha rnams ston te/_dbus su ni rje btsun dam pa shAkya thub pa gser btso ma lta bu phyag g.yas g.yon dag mchog sbyin dang /_ting nge 'dzin mdzad pa/_dge slong gi cha lugs can seng ge khri'i pad+ma dang zla bas brgyan pa la rdo rje skyil krung gis bzhugs pa'o/_/
[\[240b.4\]]shar gyi rtsibs la phyag na rdo rje mdog ljang gu dpa' bo g.yas dang g.yon na rdo rje dang dril bu 'dzin pa'o/_/lhor rgyal ba'i gtsug tor mdog sngo bsangs la/_phyag g.yas g.yon rdo rje dang rgyal mtshan 'dzin pa'o/_/nub tu 'khor los sgyur ba mdog ser po phyag gnyis na gser gyi 
[\[240b.5\]]'khor lo rtsibs brgyad pa 'dzin pa'o/_/byang du rnam par rgyal ba mdog ser po phyag g.yas g.yon gnyis na dam pa sbyin pa dang /_pad+ma gtsug tor gyis mtshan pa can no/_/shar lhor gzi brjid kyi phung po mdog dmar skya la phyag gnyis nyi ma rin po ches mtshan pa 'dzin pa'o/_/lho nub tu rnam 
[\[240b.6\]]par 'joms pa mdog sngo bsangs phyag g.yas g.yon be con dang sdigs mdzub 'dzin pa'o/_/nub byang du rnam par 'thor ba mdog dmar ba phyag g.yas g.yon na ral gri dang sdigs mdzub can no/_/byang shar du gdugs dkar po can mdog dkar po/_phyag gnyis gdugs dkar po 
[\[240b.7\]]gser gyi yu ba can 'dzin pa ste/_'di dag ni rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs pa'i cha lugs bshad pa dang 'dra ba las rgyal ba'i rigs 'ba' zhig yin pas na gtsug tor gyi ming gis mtshan pa'o/_/lha lhag ma rnams ni sngon bzhin no/_/phyag na rdo rje tshe dpag tu med pa'i dkyil 'khor 
[\[241a\]]
[\[241a.1\]]du ni dbus su phyag na rdo rje sku mdog dkar po/_phyag gnyis rdo rje dang dril bu thugs kar 'dzin pa dpa' bo'i cha byad can rgyan ma lus pas brgyan pa'o/_/phyogs bzhir rgyal po bzhi 'khor los sgyur ba'i cha lugs can te/_rtsa ba'i dkyil 'khor du ji skad du bshad 
[\[241a.2\]]pa las/_'dir gdan pad+ma dang zla ba 'ba' zhig la lhag ma ni snga ma bzhin no/_/rgyal po chen po bzhi la sogs pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi bdag po khams gsum rnam par rgyal ba ni/_ji ltar rdo rje gtsug tor du bstan pa'i phyag na rdo rje nyid las cung zad khro ba'i ngang tshul can nam/_yang na 
[\[241a.3\]]hUM gi phyag rgya rdo rje dang dril bu bcang bar bya'o/_/phyogs lhag ma ni bshad zin to/_/phyogs skyong ba'i dkyil 'khor du yang de bzhin te lhag ma ni bshad zin la/_cung zad du ni nyi ma dang phyag gnyis mdun du shar stod dang shar smad du'o/_/gza'i dkyil 'khor du yang de bzhin te/_lhag 
[\[241a.4\]]ma rnams bshad zin to/_/klu chen po rnams kyi dkyil 'khor du yang de bzhin te/_lhag ma rnams ni bshad zin to/_/'jigs byed chen po rnams kyi dkyil 'khor du ni/_dbus su khams gsum rnam par rgyal ba de nyid 'phags pa de kho na 'dus pa'i rgyud las/_ut+pal cher sngo 
[\[241a.5\]]'tsher ba'i mdog_/rdo rje hUM mdzad bsdus pa ni/_/khro dang bcas par 'dzum pa'i zhal/_/g.yon brkyang tshul du gnas pa ste/_/'bar ba'i phreng bas 'khrigs pa'i 'od/_/g.yon pa'i zhabs kyi mthil gyis ni/_/dbang phyug chen po mnan par bya/_/g.yas pa'i zhabs kyis dka' 
[\[241a.6\]]zlog gi/_/nu ma'i steng du gnon pa bri/_/zhes gsungs pa gnas pa ste/_'di ni rab tu drag po'i dkyil 'khor yin pa'i phyir ro/_/de'i mdun du ni 'jigs byed chen po ste khyu mchog la zhon pa nag po g.yas dang g.yon rtse gsum dang cang te'u thogs pa rab tu 'jigs su rung 
[\[241a.7\]]ba/_'jigs byed ma de dang 'dra ba dang bcas pa'o/_/lhor khyab 'jug mthing ga_/g.yas dang g.yon na 'khor lo dbyug pa can nam mkha' lding la zhon pa/_nub tu tshangs pa ngang pa la zhon pa gdong bzhi pa g.yas dang g.yon na spyi blugs dang bgrang phreng can no/_/shar du dbang po dkar 
[\[241b\]]
[\[241b.1\]]po sa srung gi bu la zhon pa'o/_/rdo rje dang sdigs mdzub g.yas dang g.yon pas 'dzin pa'o/_/byang shar du tshogs kyi bdag po dmar ba glang po'i gdong can dgra sta dang /_la phug g.yas dang g.yon na 'dzin pa byi ba la zhon pa'o/_/byang du lus ngan sngon po rta la zhon pa ne'u le dang rin po 
[\[241b.2\]]che'i dbyug to 'dzin pa'o/_/nub byang du b+h+r-ing+gi ri ti lus sha med pa/_keng rus su gnas pa g.yas dang g.yon na phreng ba dang ting nge 'dzin du gnas pa'o/_/lho nub tu gshin rje nag po be con dang /_sdigs mdzub g.yas dang g.yon na 'dzin pa'o/_/'dir khyab 'jug la sogs pa thams cad 
[\[241b.3\]]kyang rang rang gi phyogs 'dra ba'am/_yang ci dga' ba'i chung ma rnams kyi mgul nas 'khyud pa thams cad kyang rab tu 'jigs pa'i gzugs can du gnas pa'o/_/lhag ma ni snga ma bzhin no/_/lha chen po rnams kyi dkyil 'khor du dbus su snga ma bzhin no/_/shar du lha chen po dkar 
[\[241b.4\]]po mig gsum pa/_g.yas dag na tri shU la dang khri rkang 'dzin pa/_g.yon dag na thod pa 'dzin pa/_zla ba phyed pa dang thod pas mgo la mtshan pa khyu mchog la zhon pa'o/_/lhor dbang po ljang sngon sa srung gi bu la zhon pa g.yas dang g.yon dag rdo rje rtse gsum pa dang mchog sbyin no/_/nub 
[\[241b.5\]]tu tshangs pa dmar po g.yas dag na pad+ma dang tri shU la 'dzin pa'o/_/g.yon dag na phreng ba dang dbyug pa 'dzin pa ngang pa la gnas pa'o/_/byang du khyab 'jug sngon po g.yas dag na 'khor lo dang dung 'dzin pa/_g.yon dag na rdo rje dang dbyug pa can nam mkha' lding la zhon pa/_byang shar 
[\[241b.6\]]du tshogs kyi bdag po dmar skya g.yas dag na dgra sta dang la phug_/g.yon dag na la du can byi ba la zhon pa'o/_/shar lhor 'dod pa'i lha dmar po mda' dang gzhu thogs pa'o/_/nub byang du b+h+r-ing+gi ri ti dkar po g.yas na tri shU la/_g.yon na nor bu can no/_/lho nub tu smin drug gi bu 
[\[241b.7\]]mgo gsum pa lag gnyis mdung thogs pa'o/_/phyi rol du shar du nag po chen po mig bgrang ba thor tshugs can zla ba tshes pa dang thod pa skam pos mgo la mtshan pa'o/_/lag pa g.yas dang g.yon dang po gnyis na nyi ma dang zla ba 'dzin pa/_'og ma gnyis rtse gsum pas mtshan 
[\[242a\]]
[\[242a.1\]]pa/_seng ge'i khri la sku rten bcas te 'khod pa/_stag lpags kyis smad dkris pa mtshan bgreng ba'am/_yang na lag pa drug pa las g.yas g.yon tha ma seng ge dang khrag gis bkang ba'i thod pa 'dzin pa'o/_/lhor ni stobs [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]ljang ser sdang mig tu lta ba dang lag pa 
[\[242a.2\]]gnyis tri shU la'am bshol thogs pa'o/_/nub tu dge ba'i dbang phyug ste kha dog dmar ba gsus pa che ba nag po chen po'i mtshan ma can no/_/byang du ni stobs chen te stobs [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]lta bu las 'khor lo thogs pa lta bu'o/_/'di ltar yang rang 'dra ba'i chung mas mgul nas 
[\[242a.3\]]'khyud pa'o/_/kha cig na re 'jig rten pa'i dkyil 'khor 'di dag gi lhag ma lus pa rang rang dang 'dra ba'i chung mas 'khyud pa'o zhes zer ro/_/lhag ma rnams ni snga ma bzhin no/_/de bzhin gshegs pa tshe dpag tu med pa'i dkyil 'khor ni dbus su bcom ldan 'das tshe 
[\[242a.4\]]mtha' yas sku mdog dkar po phyag g.yon dang g.yas gnyis ting nge 'dzin dang skyabs sbyin mdzad pa/_'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po chas pa lta bu'o/_/shar du phyag na rdo rje sngon bzhin te ljang gu'am dkar po/_byang du khro bo rnam par rgyal ba mdog sngo smug g.yon pa sdigs mdzub 
[\[242a.5\]]can/_g.yas rdo rje kun du rtse gdengs pa'o/_/g.yas su 'phags pa nam mkha'i snying po phyag g.yas g.yon dag na ral gri dang rin po che 'dzin pa/_nub tu 'phags pa 'jig rten dbang phyug dmar po phyag g.yon pas pad+ma'i yu ba nas 'dzin cing g.yas pas thugs kar 
[\[242a.6\]]'byed pa'o/_/lhag ma rnams ni sngon bzhin no/_/de nas rgyud phyi ma'i phyi mar kun du bzang po la sogs pa'i dkyil 'khor du ni/_dbus su phyag na rdo rje sngon bshad pa lta bu'am/_khams gsum rnam par rgyal ba sngon bshad pa lta bu'o/_/yang na rdo rje sems dpa' rdo rje dang dril 
[\[242a.7\]]bu thugs kar 'dzin pa mdog dkar ba bsam/_kun du bzang po ting nge 'dzin gyi phyag na rdo rje rin po ches mtshan pa'am/_bde ba chen po dmar ba phyag dang po na rdo rje dang dril bu 'dzin pa/_phyag 'og ma gnyis na mda' gzhu 'gengs pa dag gang yang rung ba'o/_/mdun du phyag na 
[\[242b\]]
[\[242b.1\]]rdo rje ste ljang gu la sngon bzhin no/_/g.yas dang nub dang byang du rdo rje rin chen dang /_rdo rje chos dang rdo rje las rnams sngon bzhin no/_/rim pa gnyis pa las sangs rgyas rnam par gzigs dang /_thams cad skyob dang /_gtsug tor can dang /_'khor ba 'jig dang /_gser thub dang /_
[\[242b.2\]]'od srungs dang /_mar me mdzad dang /_shAkya'i tog dag shar nas brtsams te/_dge slong gi cha byad can spyod lam mdzes pa gser btso ma'i mdog can mnyam pa'i skyil krung la ting nge 'dzin gyi phyag rgya can no/_/rim pa gsum pa la rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs pa sngon bzhin no/_/
[\[242b.3\]]rim pa bzhi pa la byams pa la sogs pa sngon bzhin no/_/rim pa lnga pa la nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas rnams sngon bzhin no/_/rim pa drug pa la lha chen po bzhi dang /_rgyal po chen po bzhi dang phyogs skyong ba bcu gcig ste sngon bzhin no/_/rim pa bdun pa la gza' dang rgyu skar 
[\[242b.4\]]dang /_sa'i lha nas phag gi bar du ste sngon bzhin no/_/rdo rje khro bo'i dkyil 'khor du ni/_dbus su khro bo me'i nyi ma 'jigs byed kyi dkyil 'khor bdag po lta bu'o/_/'jig rten gsum snang ni sngon po g.yas dang g.yon rdo rje rtse lnga pa dang nyi ma 'dzin pa'o/_/'jig rten 
[\[242b.5\]]gsum 'dul ni ser po g.yas dang g.yon na ral gri dang sdigs mdzub bo/_/'jig rten gsum 'joms ni dmar po g.yas dang g.yon na dgra sta dang sdigs mdzub bo/_/'jig rten gsum 'ching ni lcags kyu dang lcags sgrog go/_/rdo rje bdud rtsi ni sngo ba phyag gnyis dpung par 
[\[242b.6\]]bsnol ba'o/_/dbyug pa sngon po ni sngo skya g.yas dang g.yon na be con dang sdigs mdzub zhags pa can no/_/mi g.yo ba ni smug nag g.yas g.yon rdo rje dang sdigs mdzub can no/_/rta mgrin dmar po g.yas g.yon be con dang sdigs mdzub can no/_/dus kyi lcags kyu ni dkar ba g.yas 
[\[242b.7\]]g.yon lcags kyu dang sdigs mdzub ste/_'og ma dag gis kyang g.yon pa rnams sdigs mdzub bo/_/dus kyi zhags pa sngon po g.yas na zhags pa can no/_/dus kyi 'ching ba dmar ba lcags sgrog can no/_/dus kyi chags pa ljang gu g.yas dril bu'o/_/dus kyi srin po ni 
[\[243a\]]
[\[243a.1\]]nag po g.yas g.yon be con dang sdigs mdzub can no/_/dus kyi dbyig pa ni smug nag g.yas g.yon be con dang sdigs mdzub bo/_/dus kyi gnod sbyin mo ni smug nag g.yas g.yon mche ba dang skyabs sbyin can no/_/dus kyi mtshan mo ni nag mo g.yon dgra sta 
[\[243a.2\]]dang sdigs mdzub can no/_/'di rnams kyis ji lta ba 'dod pa'i ma rungs pa rnams g.yon brkyang bas brdzis pa la/_g.yon pas sna tshogs pad+ma'i nyi ma mnan pa ste/_shar gyi rtsibs nas gzung ste'o/_/dus kyi srin mo la sogs pa ni lha'i snam bu'i gra bzhir shar lho nas brtsams te'o/_/
[\[243a.3\]]yang na phyag ni brgya phrag du mar ldan zhing bya ba dang /_rgyu ma thogs shing dbu mtha' yas/_/zhes gsungs pas/_dbyibs nges pa ci zhig bya ste/_blo la ji ltar nus pa'i dbu dang phyag dang cha lugs mi bzad par bsgom zhing bri bar bya'o/_/kha cig dgod pa gzhan du byed pa ni mi rigs te/_
[\[243a.4\]]che ba'i cho ga ste/_rtsibs bcu gnyis zhes gsungs pa'i phyir ro/_/de bzhin du dbyibs nges pa yang bsal zin to/_/sgo skyong rnams ni sngon bzhin no/_/de ltar lha'i tshogs 'di snyed kyi gnas su gyur pa'i phyir/_/lha'i mchog thams cad kyi dga' ba'i tshal yid du 'ong ba zhes bya 
[\[243a.5\]]ba gsungs te/_bcom ldan 'das thugs rje chen pos 'gro ba la phan pa'i phyir zhugs pa ni kha dog dang dbyibs dang /_rang bzhin spyod lam dang /_skyes pa dang bud med dang /_'gro ba rigs sna tshogs su snang bas ji ltar rigs par 'gro ba rnams 'dul bar mdzad pa ste/_ji skad 
[\[243a.6\]]du zla gsang thig le zhes bya ba rnal 'byor gyi rgyud las kyang /_res 'ga' 'dod dbang rgyal po ste/_/res 'ga' 'dod pa rgyab kyis phyogs/_/res 'ga' zhi ba res 'ga' drag_/res 'ga' gdon chen bgegs mdzad de/_/de la sogs te mtha' yas pa/_/phyag rgya 'di yis rgyas 
[\[243a.7\]]btab pas/_/ci dga' dngos grub 'dod pa la/_/bdag gi ngo bor ston par byed/_/ces gsungs te/_de'i phyir yang dag par na bcom ldan 'das nyid 'khor phun sum tshogs par yang snang ste/_'chad pa por ste de ni chos so/_/rang yon tan ldan pas nyan pa'ang nga zhes 
[\[243b\]]
[\[243b.1\]]gsungs pa lta bu'o/_/de ltar lha'i mchog thams cad ces bya ba'i bshad pa mchog ma brjod nas/_da ni yang tha mal pa nyid la 'jug par bya ste/_lha rnams nyid mchog yin la de rnams thams cad dga' ba'i tshal lam/_yang na sum cu rtsa gsum gyi lha rnams kyi nang na mchog 
[\[243b.2\]]ni dga' ba'i tshal na gnas pa rnams te/_de rtag tu yid dga' ba'i phyir ro/_/ci de rnams la yang tha dad pa yod dam zhe na/_los kyang ste sum cu rtsa gsum zhes bya ba nyid kyang chos bzang na gnas pa la sogs pa sum cu rtsa gsum nyid/_'phags pa dam pa'i chos dran pa nye 
[\[243b.3\]]bar gzhag pa las gsungs pa ni bstan bcos las grags pa yin/_drag po bcu gcig la sogs pa sum cu rtsa gsum ni/_'jig rten la grags pa'o/_/dga' ba'i tshal zhes bya ba ni/_grong khyer chen po blta na sdug ces bya ba'i byang phyogs dpag tshad nyi shu 'das pa na/_skyed 
[\[243b.4\]]mos tshal dga' ba zhes bya ba chur dpag tshad nyis brgya lnga bcu/_rgyar dpag tshad nyis brgya lnga bcu/_mtha' skor du dpag tshad stong yod pa ste/_gser gyi ra bas bskor ba bla gab can/_'dod pa thams cad sbyor ba/_bdud rtsi rnam pa bzhi dang /_shing dang rdzing dang /_me tog phun 
[\[243b.5\]]sum tshogs pa/_gser gyi bye ma bdal ba yod de/_de bas na yid du 'ong ba zhes bya ba nas/_lha dang lha'i bu mo sna tshogs pa rnam par rtse ba zhes bya ba'i bar du smos pa yin no/_/de ltar na dga' ba'i tshal ni ha cang ches la/_'khor dang bcas pa bcom ldan 'das rab tu yang rgya 
[\[243b.6\]]che ba ji ltar 'dzin nus she na/_de ni sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis bsnyung gi rtse tsam gyi sa phyogs kyang rab tu yangs la yid du 'ong bar 'gyur zhing /_rgyal ba zhi bar gshegs nas ni dman zhing kho re chung bar 'gyur te/_ji skad du 'phags pa sdong po bkod pa las/_gang tshe rgyal 
[\[243b.7\]]ba mya ngan 'das gyur pa/_/de tshe de yi zhing rnams 'grib par 'gyur/_/zhing gi rgya mtsho de yang nyon mongs ldan/_/sems can rnams kyang kun nas nyon mongs 'gyur/_/zhes gsungs la/_yang gzhan du gsungs pa/_skra tsam khyon la sangs rgyas mang po ni/_/gang+gA'i klung gi 
[\[244a\]]
[\[244a.1\]]bye ma ji snyed pa/_/rgyal ba de rnams zhing yang de snyed de/_/de yang skal ba mi mnyam mtshan nyid bcas/_/skra tsam khyon 'dir 'gro ba lngar gtogs pa/_/dmyal ba rnams dang dud 'gro'i 'gro ba dang /_/gshin rje'i 'jig rten lha dang mi rnams kyang /_/dogs pa dag 
[\[244a.2\]]dang nye bar gtser ba 'ang med/_/de ltar rgyal ba'i chos de bsam mi khyab/_/ces so/_/de bas na 'phags pa sdom pa gsum bstan pa las/_ri'i rgyal po bya rgod kyi phung po de ni mtho ba zlum pa gnas 'phang dang rgyar yangs pa zhes bya ba la sogs pa bstan pa yang sdud pa pos legs 
[\[244a.3\]]par smras pa ste/_ri de yang sangs rgyas bzhugs shing 'tsho ba de na de ltar bden la/_deng sang na ni ri rab tu chung bar snang bas so/_/yang chos thams cad gzugs brnyan ltar ma shes shing /_bsod nams ma byas pa la dman zhing tha gru chung la/_de dag bsod nams byas pa 
[\[244a.4\]]dang /_chos thams cad gzugs brnyan ltar shes pa dag la/_rab tu yangs shing bzang la 'byor par snang ba ni/_ji ltar 'phags pa dri ma med par grags pas bstan pa zhes bya ba'i mdo las/_tshangs pa ral pa can gyis/_'phags pa shA ri'i bu la smras pa/_btsun pa shA ri'i 
[\[244a.5\]]bu de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di yongs su ma dag go zhes ma zer cig_/btsun pa shA ri'i bu bcom ldan 'das kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag pa 'di lta ste/_dper na btsun pa shA ri'i bu gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi lha'i gnas bkod pa bzhin du/_bcom ldan 
[\[244a.6\]]'das shAkya thub pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing bkod pa yang nged kyis de 'dra bar mthong ngo zhes gsungs pa lta bu'o/_/de ltar na dga' ba'i tshal de'i me tog la sogs pa ma lus pa bcom ldan 'das kyi byin gyi rlabs yin nam zhe na/_lha de dag gi rang gi las las byung ba yang yod de/_

[\[249a.2\]]goms pa'i sa dag la/_/byang chub sems dpa' gang dag gnas/_/de 'dir phyir mi ldog pa'i tshogs/_/zhes gsungs pas so/_/de nyid mi phyed pas na dge 'dun te/_ji skad du/_sangs rgyas chos dang dge 'dun rnams/_/bdud rnams bye ba brgya yis kyang /_/bskal 
[\[249a.3\]]pa brgyar yang mi phyed de/_/de nas dge 'dun zhes brjod do/_/zhes gsungs so/_/mtha' thug pa med pa rnams kyis zhes bya ba ni/_rab tu yang mang bas so so'i skye bos grangs kyi mi rtogs pas so/_/'o na nyan thos kyi dge 'dun ci'i phyir ma smos she na/_'dir theg pa chen 
[\[249a.4\]]po kho na ston pas de dag snod du ma gyur pas ma 'dus so zhes kha cig zer ro/_/de lta na theg pa chen po 'ba' zhig ston pa'i rgyud gzhan las kyang mi smos par rigs na smos pa yod do/_/kha cig na re 'di ni rtsa ba'i rgyud bshad ma thag tu gsungs pas/_sprul pa'i skus don mdzad 
[\[249a.5\]]pa'i gang yin pas/_'phags pa nyan thos kyi dge 'dun med do zhes zer te/_de ni rang gi rtog pa'i dri ma stug pos khyab par zad do/_/'dir gtsor theg pa chen po bstan pa'i skabs yin pas na/_'phags pa nyan thos rnams ma smos kyang nyes pa med pa la dgongs nas ma smos 
[\[249a.6\]]par zad kyi/_dgra bcom pa chen po rnams kyang nges par 'dus par blo dang ldan pa rnams kyis shes par bya'o/_/de la 'dir ci'i phyir 'jig rten pa'i 'khor dang por smos she na/_sdud pa po thugs rje che bas 'jig rten pa rnams spro ba skyed pa'i ched du'o zhes kha cig zer te/_de lta 
[\[249a.7\]]na gzhung gzhan du/_sdud pa po'i thugs rje chung bar 'gyur ro/_/kha cig na re rgyud 'di ni gzhan gyi don lhur len pa yin la/_'jig rten pa'i 'khor dang smos pa yang thugs rjes gzhan gyi don gtsor byed pa'o zhes zer te/_'jig rten las 'das pa dang smos na/_gzhan 
[\[249b\]]
[\[249b.1\]]gyi don bya ba la gnod pa ci yod de 'di ni gyi nar zad do/_/kha cig na re 'jig rten pa'i lam la brten nas 'jig rten las 'das pa'i lam skye'o zhes [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]nges par shes par bya ba'i phyir ro zhes zer ro/_/de yang cung zad mi 'thad de/_'dir lam gyi [{]go rims kyi [,]go rim gyi [}]skabs la ma 
[\[249b.2\]]bab pas so/_/'di ni dbang po la sogs pa'i lha gnyug mar gnas pa de dag dang lam brjod par glo bur ba'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams smos na/_lha'i bu bden par ma mthong ba rnams yid mi dga' bar 'gyur ba'i ngo bsrung ba'i phyir/_gnyug mar gnas pa rnams dang por 
[\[249b.3\]]smos so zhes lo rgyus kyis bshad do/_/de ltar na 'gro ba tha dad pa de dag ci'i phyir dang por bya zhe na/_de ni 'jig rten pa'i 'khor du 'dra bas/_lha'i rnams kyi zhar la bstan par rigs pa'i phyir ro/_/bsti stang du byed ces bya ba ni lus kyi las kyis so/_/bla mar byed ces bya 
[\[249b.4\]]ba ni yid la gcags par lta ba'o/_/mchod cing zhes bya ba ni rnyed pa'i ngo bo me tog la sogs pas so/_/brjod ces bya ba ni ngag gi las kyi bstod pa don dang mthun par byed pa'o/_/rab tu mchod cing zhes bya ba ni bka' bzhin du byed pa ste 'di ni mchod pa'i mchog yin pas so/_/spel 
[\[249b.5\]]te zhes bya ba ni mchod pa'i dge ba rnams byang chub thob pa'i rgyur yongs su bsngos pa ste/_'di'i ni mi zad cing 'phel ba'i mchog yin te/_'phags pa byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod kyi mdo las/_rgya mtsho chen por 'pho ba'i chu ni bar ma dor zad par mi 'gyur ba de bzhin du 
[\[249b.6\]]byang chub tu bsngos pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba yang bar ma dor zad par mi 'gyur te zhes gsungs pas so/_/'khor mang po'i dbus na zhes bya ba ni/_sngar 'das ma thag pa rnams kyi dkyil na'o/_/tshangs pa chen po zhes bya ba ni bshad zin to/_/pad+ma'i gdan la zhes bya ba ni/_'dir 
[\[249b.7\]]bcom ldan 'das 'khor ba na bzhugs kyang de'i nyes pas mi gos par ston pa'i phyir pad+ma'i gdan la bzhugs pa ste/_rten gyis brten pa bstan pa'i phyir ro/_/de yang ji ltar dpal mchog dang po las gsungs pa/_ji ltar pad+ma rab dmar yang /_/tshon gyi nyes pas mi gos pa/_/de bzhin gnas 
[\[250a\]]
[\[250a.1\]]pa'i nyes pas kyang /_/'gro phan rnams la mi gos so/_/zhes so/_/bzhugs zhes bya ba ni 'phags pa dang tshangs pa dang /_lha'i bzhugs pa las ni 'dir tshangs pa'i bzhugs pa ste/_thugs rje chen pos ngan song gi rgyud sbyong ba'i phyir ro/_/spyod lam dang nang du 
[\[250a.2\]]yang dag 'jog dang /_chos 'chad pa'i bzhugs pa las ni/_chos 'chad pa'i bzhugs pa ste/_'dir de gtsor gyur pa'i phyir ro zhes bya ba ni/_'das pa ston pa ste/_yang dag pa bsdus pa'i dus las/_de sngon yin pa'i phyir ro/_/gleng gzhi rgya cher bshad pa'o/_/de ltar 
[\[250a.3\]]nges pa bskyed pa'i gleng gzhi brjod pas/_yid ches par 'gyur ba'i rjes las rgyud kyi don ji ltar nye bar phye snyam pa'i rtog pa zlog par byed pa/_gleng bslang ba bstan pa/_ngan song gi rgyud thams cad yongs su sbyong ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/_
[\[250a.4\]]ngan song gi rgyud las rnam par grol bar byed ces bya ba la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i ting nge 'dzin gyi 'od zer sdud pa mang po'i phreng ba nyid kyis dpral ba'i mdzod spu nas phyung nas zhes bya ba la sogs pa smos te/_'dir ting nge 'dzin dang po ni de bzhin 
[\[250a.5\]]gshegs pa ngan song thams cad sbyong ba'i gzugs mngon du mdzad pa yin la/_gnyis pa ni byang chub sems dpa' ngan song thams cad yongs su sbyong ba zhes bya ba khams gsum rnam par rgyal ba'i ngo bo mngon sum du mdzad pa yin te/_'od zer gyi 'phro ba dang 'du ba ni 'dra bar ngan 
[\[250a.6\]]song thams cad yongs su sbyong bar byed pa la sogs pa yin no/_/de bas na rnal 'byor pa bdag gis kyang dang por de bzhin gshegs pa'i skur gyur pas sbyin sreg dang po bya la/_khams gsum rnam par rgyal ba'i skur gnas te/_sbyin sreg gnyis pa bya'o zhes bya ba ni spyi'i 
[\[250a.7\]]don te/_yan lag gi don ni ngan song zhes bya ba ni sngar brjod pa rnams so/_/de dag gi rgyud ni rang gi las kyi 'bras bu ma zad kyi bar mi chad par 'jug pa'o zhes bya ba ni tshig gi mtshams sbyor ba dang /_ming gi mtha' phrad do/_/ting nge 'dzin la zhes bya ba ni/_thugs rtse gcig 
[\[250b\]]
[\[250b.1\]]par gnas pa la'o/_/gdul ba zhi ba dag gi ched du/_sangs rgyas kyi ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/_gang dag ma rung ba 'dul ba de dag gi don ni rdo rje khro bo nyid kyis ni mdzad par rigs pas na/_ngan song gi rgyud las zhes bya ba la sogs pa smos te sngar bshad pa bzhin no/_/
[\[250b.2\]]rnam par grol bar byed pa ste/_sangs rgyas su'am yang na sna tshogs par grol bar byed pa ni rnam par grol bar byed de/_re zhig rigs nges pa 'phags pa rnga bo che'i mdo las gdul ba'i rim pas gsungs pa bzhin no zhes bya ba ni sngar bstan pa lta bu'o/_/byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[250b.3\]]sems dpa' chen po zhes bya ba ni bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten gsum las rnam par rgyal ba ste/_tshig gi don ni sngar bshad pa bzhin no/_/ting nge 'dzin zhes bya ba ni sngar bshad pa bzhin te/_snyoms par zhugs nas zhes 'dir yang sbyar bar bya'o/_/'od zer sdud pa mang po'i 
[\[250b.4\]]phreng ba zhes bya ba la/_ye shes las byung ba'i snang ba'i 'od zer yin la/_bems po las byung ba'i snang ba ni 'od do/_/yang na lus las byung ba'i snang ba ni 'od yin la/_sems las byung ba'i snang ba ni 'od zer yin te/_de bas na 'dir 'od ces bya ba smos so/_/ngan song 
[\[250b.5\]]rnams mtho ris la sogs par sdud pas na/_sdud ces smos te/_'chad par gyur pa las dga' ba'i tshal thams cad kun du snang bar byas te zhes bya ba la sogs pa smos pas so/_/phreng ba zhes bya ba ni phreng ba ltar mdzes pa'am/_'od zer de dag phreng bar sbrengs shing bar 
[\[250b.6\]]chad med pa'o/_/nyid kyi zhes bya ba ni bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' bdag nyid kyi'o/_/mdzod spu nas zhes bya ba ni smin mtshams nas dung dang kha ba ltar dkar ba'i spu sngon bla ma'i skye bo rnams gnas ji lta bar bkod pa las grub pa'i mtshan yod de/_de nyid 'od zer 
[\[250b.7\]]las byung ba mi zad pa'i mdzod yin no/_/gdul bya rnams phyir mi ldog par 'god par mdzad pa'i 'od zer ni gnas 'di nas 'byin to/_/byung nas zhes bya ba ni mngon par 'thon pa ste/_'dir slar bsdu ba'i gnas kyang de nyid yin par shes par bya'o/_/des stong gsum gyi stong 
[\[251a\]]
[\[251a.1\]]chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams zhes bya ba nas/_byang chub spyod pa nyid la gzhag_/ces bya ba'i bar du ni 'od zer gyi las bstan pa ste/_sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyi rmi lam ltar yud cig grol ba la/_yun [{]rings su [,]ring du [}]grol bar 'du shes pa'am/_
[\[251a.2\]]skal ba dang ldan pa dag gtan grol ba'o/_/tshig gi don ji lta bu zhe na/_de la stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams zhes bya ba ni/_ji skad du chos mngon pa las/_gling bzhi pa dang nyi zla dang /_/ri rab dang ni 'dod lha dang /_/tshangs pa'i 'jig rten stong la ni/_/
[\[251a.3\]]stong ni spyi phud yin par 'dod/_/de stong la ni stong gnyis pa/_/bar ma'i 'jig rten khams zhes bya/_/de stong la ni stong gsum mo/_/mnyam du 'jig cing 'byung ba yin/_/zhes bshad ste/_skye bo blo chung dag gi ngor rje btsun shAkya thub pa'i zhing ni de tsam zhig tu zad do zhes bstan 
[\[251a.4\]]pas zhing rgya chen ma smos so/_/kun du snang bar byas so zhes bya ba ni/_gang na mun nag smag gis khyab pa'i nyi ma dang zla ba gnyis kyang mi rgyu ba'i khor yug gi bar de dag mun pa'i g.yung drung ste/_ji skad du 'jig rten gdags pa las/_rgya mtsho 'di yi pha rol na/_/gang gis 
[\[251a.5\]]rgya mtsho bskor ba yi/_/khor yug dpag tshad sum brgya dang /_/dpag tshad phyed dang gsum yod do/_/de ni khor yug rir bshad de/_/'jig rten bar mtshams tshun chad du/_/nyi zla de bzhin skar ma dang /_/rgyu skar tshogs ni rgya chen kun/_/khor yug mi 'da' 'jig rten 
[\[251a.6\]]gyi/_/bar mtshams phan chad mun par 'dug_/ces gsungs pas so/_/de yang snang bar byas pas sems can de dag 'di skad du/_kye ma 'dir sems can gzhan skyes so zhes smra bar 'gyur bar byas pa'o/_/ci mun pa gsal ba tsam las mi 'da' byas sam snyam pa 
[\[251a.7\]]las/_kun du snang bar byas nas sems can thams cad kyi sems nyon mongs pas bcings pa las rnam par bkrol te zhes bya ba gsungs te/_nyon mongs pa nyid bcings pa ste lhag ma ni go bar sla'o/_/ci'i de tsam du zad snyam pa las/_bde ba so sor thob nas zhes 
[\[251b\]]
[\[251b.1\]]gsungs te/_so sor thob nas zhes bya ba ni/_rang rang gi 'dod pa ji lta ba bzhin du rdzogs zhes bya ba'i don to/_/de dag las gang dag lhar skyes pa de dag ni sngon gyi gnas dang lhar skye ba'i rgyu dran nas/_sangs rgyas la byas pa shes par bya ba'i phyir mchod pa byed du lhags pas 
[\[251b.2\]]na/_dga' ba'i tshal zhes bya ba la sogs pa smos te go sla'o/_/nor bu dri ma med pa'i steng du 'khod de zhes bya ba la dga' ba'i tshal de'i gzhi nor bu gser dri ma med pa'i bye ma bdal ba yin pas de la 'khod nas so/_/gser ni nor bu ma yin gyi/_rin po che yin no zhe na/_
[\[251b.3\]]yid dga' bar byed pa'i phyir nor bu zhes bya ba la/_gser yang yid dga' bar byed pa'am nor bu'i rgyur gnas pa'i phyir de skad ces bya'o/_/yang na nor bu dri ma med pa'i zhes bya ba ni/_lha'i bu 'das nas zhag bdun lon pa gang yin pa'o/_/steng du zhes bya ba ni de lha'i 
[\[251b.4\]]gnas su skyes pa'i sngon du de dag gi gnas su skyes nas der 'khod pa ste/_de bas na bcom ldan 'das lha'i bu nor bu dri ma med pa'i ched du der bzhugs kyang /_de nyid kyi las kyi 'bras bu nges par myong bar bya ba cung zad tsam pa de dus ma rdzogs par ston pas/_'gro 
[\[251b.5\]]ba rnams las la 'dzem par bstan pa ste/_rgyal po ma skyes dgra stong pa nyid chen por rtogs kyang mtshams med pa'i las kyi dbang gis dar gyi pho long brdabs pa tsam zhig mnar med par gnas par gsungs pa bzhin no/_/de bas na bcom ldan 'das brgya byin dang 
[\[251b.6\]]bcas pa ni re zhig lha'i bu de 'dren pa'i rkyen tsam du zad de/_'gro ba rnams ni las bdag gir bya ba la/_las kyi bgo skal la spyod pa'o/_/gzhan du 'gro ba ma lus pa thugs rje'i dbang gis ji ltar mi gnas pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa la mi 'god/_de ltar na 'brel ba 
[\[251b.7\]]med pa'i phyir dang /_ma byas pa dang phrad pa'i ni med la byas pa ni chud mi za'o zhes gsungs pas/_rkyen nyid kyang bya bar mi nus so zhe na/_'di'i don ni 'og nas brjod par bya'o/_/'di skad ces gsol to zhes bya ba ni ched du brjod pa ste/_rab tu ngo mtshar che bas na spro ba'i 
[\[252a\]]
[\[252a.1\]]tshul gyis/_e ma sangs rgyas zhes lan gsum bos pa'o/_/mdzad pa legs zhes bshad pa ni/_gang phyir ngan song thams cad sbyangs/_/zhes bya ba dang /_byang chub spyod pa nyid la gzhag ces bya'o/_/de nas/_lha yi dbang po brgya byin gyis/_/zhes bya 
[\[252a.2\]]ba'i bar nas/_bde bar gshegs pa ngo mtshar che'o/_/zhes bya ba'i bar du ni go sla'o/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_/zhes bya ba nas/_'di ni ngo mtshar mi che'o/_/zhes bya ba'i bar yang go sla'o/_/lha'i dbang po sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni 
[\[252a.3\]]tshad med pa'i bsod nams kyi tshogs yang dag par bsags pa'o zhes bya ba ni bskal pa grangs med gsum du/_sbyin pa dang /_tshul khrims dang /_bzod pa la bslabs pa'o/_/lha'i dbang po yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams ni/_yon tan rin po che 
[\[252a.4\]]tshad med pa'i 'byung gnas su gyur pa'o zhes bya ba ni/_dbul po rnams nor dang ldan pa dang /_gcer bu rnams gos thob pa dang /_bkres pa dang skom pa rnams zas dang skom thob pa dang /_nad pa rnams nad sos pa la sogs pa pha rol tu phyin pa gsum gyi 'bras bu 
[\[252a.5\]]'byung ba rgyun mi chad pa'o/_/lha'i dbang po yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams ni/_tshad med pa'i thabs mkhas pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'o zhes bya ba ni thabs dang /_stobs dang /_smon lam dang /_ye shes kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bskal pa grangs med pa 
[\[252a.6\]]gsum du bsags pa'o/_/lha'i dbang po sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni spyod pa tshad med pa'o zhes bya ba ni/_bsdu ba'i dngos po bzhi dang /_brtson 'grus dang /_bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa la bskal pa grangs med pa gsum du bslabs pas so/_/lha'i 
[\[252a.7\]]dbang po sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni/_'dul ba'i skye bo tshad med pa rnams snod du gyur par mdzad pa'o zhes bya ba ni/_tha na rigs chad pa rnams kyang mtho ris su dgod par spyod pas so/_/lha'i dbang po sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das ni/_mi mnyam pa dang mnyam 
[\[252b\]]
[\[252b.1\]]pa'i ye shes dang ldan pa'o zhes bya ba ni/_chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus med pa dang mnyam pa'i ye shes mnga' bas so/_/lha'i dbang po sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i rdzu 'phrul dang ldan pa'o zhes bya ba ni/_nyan thos dang rang sangs 
[\[252b.2\]]rgyas rnams dang thun mong ma yin la/_sangs rgyas rnams nyi tshe dang mnyam pa'i rdzu 'phrul mnga' bas so/_/lha yi dbang po sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni/_mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i smon lam gyi stobs dang ldan pa'o zhes bya ba ni/_sa bcu'i byang chub sems dpa'i 
[\[252b.3\]]yang yul ma yin gyi/_sangs rgyas rnams kho na'i yul du gyur pa'i smon lam lhun gyis grub ste/_ji skad du dpal sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam par sbyor ba las/_smon lam grub pas 'gag pa med pa'i chos mchog can/_/zhes gsungs so/_/de bzhin du 'phags pa sdong po bkod 
[\[252b.4\]]pa las kyang /_sngon chad smon lam mdzad pas gzugs kyi skur yang snang /_/zhes gsungs so/_/lha'i dbang po de lta bas na/_sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis snod ji lta ba bzhin du sems can gyi don mdzad pa dang /_ji ltar 'dul ba bzhin du sems can gyi don mdzad pa dang /_mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du 
[\[252b.5\]]sems can gyi don mdzad pa yin par shes par bya ba ste zhes bya ba ni dbang po'i rim pa dang /_skye ba tha dad pa la dga' ba dang /_gzugs tha dad pa la dga' ba rnams de bzhin du sbyor ba ste/_de ltar yang rnam par snang mdzad mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa las gsungs pa/_de nas yang bcom 
[\[252b.6\]]ldan 'das la/_gsang ba'i bdag po phyag na rdo rje 'khor 'dus pa de nyid na 'dug pas 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das ji ltar de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes brnyes nas 
[\[252b.7\]]thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes de sems can rnams 'phye ste/_tshul sna tshogs dang bsam pa sna tshogs dang thabs sna tshogs dag gis ston cing /_kha cig la ni nyan thos dag gi theg pa'i tshul/_kha cig la ni rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa'i tshul/_kha cig la ni theg 
[\[253a\]]
[\[253a.1\]]pa chen po'i tshul/_kha cig la ni mngon par shes pa lnga'i tshul/_kha cig la ni lhar skye bar bya ba dang /_kha cig la ni mir skye bar bya ba nas/_kha cig la ni lto 'phye chen po dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_dri za dang /_lha ma yin dang /_nam mkha' lding dang /_
[\[253a.2\]]mi'am cir skye bar bya ba'i bar du chos ston la/_de la sems can la las sangs rgyas kyis gdul bar bya ba dag gis ni/_sangs rgyas kyi gzugs su mthong /_la las ni nyan thos kyi gzugs su mthong /_la las ni rang sangs rgyas kyi gzugs su/_la las ni byang chub 
[\[253a.3\]]sems dpa'i gzugs su/_la las ni dbang phyug chen po'i gzugs su/_la las ni tshangs pa'i gzugs su/_la las ni sred med kyi bu'i gzugs su/_la las ni rnam thos kyi bu'i gzugs su/_la las ni lto 'phye chen po dang /_mi dang mi ma yin bar du mthong ste/_
[\[253a.4\]]rang rang gi tshig brjod pa'i tshul dag dang spyod lam sna tshogs su mthong la/_thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes de yang 'di ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par grol ba'i ror ro gcig pas lags zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs so/_/'di la the tshom dang som nyi dang yid 
[\[253a.5\]]gnyis ma byed cig ces bya ba ni tshad ma rnam gsum gyi yul la'am/_thos pa la sogs pa'i shes rab gsum gyi dus sam/_'jug pa la sogs pa'i spyod pa gsum gyi bdag nyid la the tshom la sogs pa spangs shig ces bya'o/_/yang na 'dir the tshom ni bstan pa yin la/_som 
[\[253a.6\]]nyi dang yid gnyis ni rnam grangs kyi bshad pa'o/_/ci'i phyir the tshom la sogs pa mi bya snyam pa la/_gang de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis 'dul bar mi 'gyur ba ni gnas ma yin no zhes bya ba smos te/_de'i rgyu mtshan yang rnam pa du ma sngar bshad zin to/_/de ltar gleng bslang ba bshad 
[\[253a.7\]]nas/_da ni gleng [(]bslar [,]bslang [)]mgo drangs pa'i yongs su shes par bya ba rgyud kyi dngos po bshad de/_de nas lha rnams kyi dbang po brgya byin zhes bya ba nas brtsams te 'di skad ces gsol to zhes bya ba'i bar du smos te go sla'o/_/nyan thos la sogs pa'i rigs can rnam pa lnga'i dbang ngam 
[\[253b\]]
[\[253b.1\]]yang na las dang po dang /_ye shes cung zad babs pa dang /_ye shes la dbang thob pa dang /_ye shes yang dag par dbang thob pa rnams gong nas gong du sangs rgyas kyi sa thob par zhes bya ba'i phyir ram/_dmyal ba dang dud 'gro dang /_yi dwags dang /_mi rnams dang lha rnams la phan 
[\[253b.2\]]gdags pa'i phyir/_sems can thams cad la phan pa nyid du bgyi ba dang zhes pa nas/_bde bar gshegs pa bdag spobs pa bskyed du gsol zhes bya ba'i bar du smos te/_de ltar las spobs pa bskyed pa ni/_shes rab kyi 'bras bu so sor smra ba la thogs pa med par bya'o/_/

[\[281b.1\]]brkyang ba'i tshul gyis 'dug la/_spyan drang ba'i phyag rgya las/_mdzub mo brkyang ba ni slar btang ba'i phyag rgya'o/_/de nas 'di'i sngags ni na mo badzra sa tsa di shi badzra pA Ni rak+Sha rak+Sha swA hA/_me'i phyogs su kha bltas nas/_lag pa g.yas pa'i mdzub mo zlum por 
[\[281b.2\]]bkug ste/_gung mo'i tshigs gsum par btsugs la/_mthe bo lag mthil du gzhag pa ni me lha spyan drang ba'i phyag rgya'o/_/dgug pa'i phyag rgya las mthe bong ngos la mdzub mo btsugs pa ni/_me lha'i dam tshig gi phyag rgya'o/_/phyag rgya de nyid la 
[\[281b.3\]]mdzub mo mthe bo'i ngos la btsugs te phyir drag tu brkyang ba ni slar btang ba'i phyag rgya'o/_/de'i sngags ni ag+ne a ha ka pa li dz+wa la dz+wa la/_da ha ni khi tho li bi rU pAk+Sha swA hA/_gshin rje'i phyogs su bltas nas snyim pa mdun du bzhag ste/_sor mo phan tshun bsnol la 
[\[281b.4\]]khu tshur bcangs nas/_srin lag gnyis bsgrengs te gshibs la g.yabs te/_nang du gzhug pa ni gshin rje dgug pa'i phyag rgya'o/_/srin lag gnyis phyi rol du shin tu gshibs te bsgrengs la snying gar gzhag pa ni dam tshig gi phyag rgya'o/_/'di nyid las 
[\[281b.5\]]srin lag sbyar ba ni slar btang ba'i phyag rgya'o/_/sngags ni sa ma ya swA hA/_bden bral gyi phyogs su rkang pa mnyam par 'dug la/_lag pa g.yas kyi khu tshur bcangs pa las/_mdzub mo dang gung mo ral gri 'dra bar gshibs la bsgreng ste gzhag_/lag pa 
[\[281b.6\]]g.yon pa'ang de ltar byas te sta zur phyogs su bzhag la g.yon pa'i mdzub mo bkug ste g.yab pa ni bden bral dgug pa'i phyag rgya'o/_/phyag rgya de nyid las lag pa g.yon pa sta zur phyogs su ral gri 'dra bar gzhag pa ni bden bral gyi dam tshig gi phyag rgya'o/_/
[\[281b.7\]]dgug pa'i phyag rgya las mdzub mo brkyang ba ni slar btang ba'i phyag rgya'o/_/sngags ni sarba b+hud+d+ha ba ya ka ya ku ru swA hA/_chu lha'i phyogs su rkang pa mnyam par 'dug la/_lag pa g.yas pa khu tshur bcangs la mthe bo dang /_mdzub mo gcig tu sbyar la/_zhags mig 
[\[282a\]]
[\[282a.1\]]ltar byas te g.yon pa'i khu tshur yang de ltar byas la snying gar bzhag ste/_g.yon pa'i mdzub mo lcags kyu 'dra bar byas pa ni dgug pa'i phyag rgya'o/_/phyag rgya de nyid la g.yon pa'i mdzub mo khu tshur gyi tshul du bzhag pa las zhags mig ltar byas pa 
[\[282a.2\]]ni chu lha'i dam tshig gi phyag rgya'o/_/g.yon pa'i phyag rgya las mdzub mo slar btang ba ni slar btang ba'i phyag rgya'o/_/sngags ni ti ti ba ti ti ti shi khi tu li bi rU pAk+Sha ya swA hA/_rlung lha'i phyogs su bltas te 'dug la/_lag pa g.yon pa khu tshur bcangs te/_gung mo bsgreng 
[\[282a.3\]]ba'i tshigs dang mdzub mo bsgreng ba'i thor to btsugs la lag pa g.yas pa de bzhin du byas te/_sta zur logs su gzhag cing mthe bong bsgul ba ni rlung lha spyan drang ba'i phyag rgya'o/_/de nyid las mthe bo snga ma bzhin du gzhag pa ni rlung lha'i dam tshig gi phyag rgya'o/_/
[\[282a.4\]]spyan drang ba'i phyag rgya las mthe bong brkyang ba ni slar btang ba'i phyag rgya'o/_/sngags ni oM swa sa kha kha kha khu khu khu swA hA/_lus ngan la mngon du bltas te 'dug nas lag pa gnyis thal mo sbyar la rdo rje bcing pa nang du bzlog ste/_mthe'u chung gnyis gshibs la 
[\[282a.5\]]brkyang /_de'i rgyab tu srin lag gnyis so sor bzhag nas gung mo gnyis de ltar bkug ste g.yab pa ni lus ngan spyan drang ba'i phyag rgya'o/_/phyag rgya de nyid las gung mo gnyis nang du rdo rje bcing ba'i tshul du gzhag pa ni/_lus ngan gyi dam tshig gi phyag rgya'o/_/'gugs 
[\[282a.6\]]pa'i phyag rgya las gung mo gnyis brkyang ba ni slar btang ba'i phyag rgya'o/_/sngags ni ku bi rA ya swA hA/_dbang ldan gyi phyogs su bltas te 'dug la/_lag pa thal mo gcig tu sbyar ba byas la/_srin lag dang mthe'u chung nang du bkug ste bsnol/_gung mo gshibs te 
[\[282a.7\]]bsgreng ba'i rgyab logs su mdzub mo gnyis rdo rje 'dra bar bya/_mthe bong gnyis bar du gshibs te bzhag la/_de nyid la mdzub mo gnyis phan tshun rtse mo sprad nas nang du g.yab pa ni dbang ldan dgug pa'i phyag rgya'o/_/de nyid las snga ma ltar mdzub mo rdo rje'i 
[\[282b\]]
[\[282b.1\]]tshul du gzhag pa ni dbang ldan gyi dam tshig gi phyag rgya'o/_/dgug pa'i phyag rgya las mdzub mo brkyang ba ni slar btang ba'i phyag rgya'o/_/sngags ni dzruM dzruM shi ba swA hA/_g.yon brkyang ba'i gnas pas 'dug pas/_lag pa thal mo khong stong du sbyar te bzhag la/_steng du bltas 
[\[282b.2\]]te mdzub mo gnyis kyi lcags kyus tshangs pa la sogs pa dgug par bya'o/_/de nyid las snga ma ltar gzhag pa ni dam tshig gi phyag rgya'o/_/dgug pa'i phyag rgya las mdzub mo gnyis brkyang ba ni slar btang ba'i phyag rgya'o/_/sngags ni 'di yin te a dar ya pra ha ma ne swA hA/_
[\[282b.3\]]sUr+ya gra ha a d+hi pa ta ye swA hA/_tsan+d+ra ya nak+Sha tra a d+hi pa ta ye swA hA/_rkang pa mnyam par gnas pas 'dug la/_lag pa gnyis gcig tu sbyar te sor mo rnams bar mtshams med par rtse mor phan tshun sbyar te/_mthe bo zlum por byas la 'og tu bltas te sa'i lha mo la sogs 
[\[282b.4\]]pa mdzub mo lcags kyu lta bur bkug pa ni 'og gi lha dgug pa'i phyag rgya'o/_/dgug pa'i phyag rgya las snga ma bzhin du mdzub mo gzhag pa ni dam tshig gi phyag rgya'o/_/mdzub mo gnyis btang ba ni slar btang ba'i phyag rgya'o/_/sngags ni a d+hi pa ti b+h+yaHswA hA/_a su re b+h+yaHswA hA/_na ge 
[\[282b.5\]]b+h+yaHswA hA/_de nas rang rang gi sngags de nyid kyis 'thor 'thung phul la/_bdag dang slob ma'i tshogs su bcas pa la bgegs med par mdzod cig_/las dang dngos grub rnams bdag cag la stsol cig ces brjod cing slar gtang ngo /_/de ltar bdag gis rgya 
[\[282b.6\]]cher bkod pa'i gtor ma 'di dag ni gang dga' bas slob ma dbang bskur ba dang /_ro yongs su sbyang ba la sogs pa'i tshe sbyin no/_/de nas sngags pas bla med byang chub kyi gnas su slob ma yongs su smin par bya zhes bya ba la sogs pa la slob ma sta gon la gnas pa ston 
[\[282b.7\]]te/_de nas zhes bya ba ni don gyis na bum pa sta gon la gnas ma thag tu'am slob ma smin par bya ba'i dbang du byas pa zhes so/_/sngags pas zhes bya ba ni mtshan nyid dang ldan pa'i slob dpon gyis so/_/bla med byang chub ces bya ba ni/_mi gnas pa'i mya ngan las 'das 
[\[283a\]]
[\[283a.1\]]pa'o/_/de nyid 'khor ba ji srid du gnas par bya bas na gnas su'o/_/slob ma zhes bya ba ni bla ma la gus pa'i yon tan dang ldan pa'o/_/smin par bya zhes bya ba ni dngos grub kyi myu gu 'byin nus par byas zhes pa'o/_/ji ltar bya zhe na/_'gro la phan phyir myur du 
[\[283a.2\]]byang chub par/_/de dag byang chub sems mchog bskyed du gzhug_/ces smos so/_/slob ma sta gon la gnas par bya ba ni/_'phags pa dkyil 'khor spyi'i bya ba las kyang /_de nas slob ma legs brtags nas/_/sta gon gnas la tshul bzhin du/_/de yi nub mo ci nus 
[\[283a.3\]]par/_/de bzhin sdom pa 'dzin du gzhug_/gtsang ma'i gos dkar bgos pa yis/_/slob ma de lta bu dag ni/_/shar phyogs kha bltas 'khod pa la/_/sta gon gnas pa brtsam par bya/_/dang por gsum la skyabs su btang /_/de nas bla mas byang chub sems/_/
[\[283a.4\]]ma bskyed pa ni bskyed par bya/_/bskyed pa dag kyang dran par bya/_/zhes gsungs pas so/_/de la 'di slob ma sta gon la gnas pa'i rim pa rgyas pa 'di yin te/_dang por slob ma legs par brtags pa smyung ba la gnas pa/_rgyan gyis brgyan pa rnams dkyil 'khor 
[\[283a.5\]]gyi gnas su shar du blta bar rgan pa'i rim gyis dgod de/_lag na me tog thogs pa rnams la slob dpon gyis chos bstan te/_de ltar yang /_yid dga' spro chen byas pa gzo/_/ngar 'dzin mi 'byed rigs mtho zhing /_/yon tan dang ldan dad dang bcas/_/
[\[283a.6\]]gzugs dang kha dog lang tshor ldan/_/bde bar gshegs pa'i gsang ba ni/_/sgrub pa don gnyer mngon brtson pa'am/_/gzugs ngan yon tan med pa'am/_/dman pa 'am ni lhag par bya/_/rang gi bslab dang legs ldan zhing /_/theg chen la dga'i bdag nyid 
[\[283a.7\]]can/_/'khor ni rnam pa bzhi char la'ang /_/dkyil 'khor cho ga rjes su gnang /_/kha cig gsang sngags bsgrub pa'i phyir/_/'di ni dkyil 'khor 'jug par byed/_/bsod nams 'dod pa de las gzhan/_/'jig rten pha rol don gnyer gzhan/_/'jig rten 
[\[283b\]]
[\[283b.1\]]pha rol gnyer ba yi/_/dad pa mang du byed pa 'di/_/blo ldan dkyil 'khor 'jug par bya'i/_/'di yi 'bras bu 'dod pas min/_/'di la 'dod par gyur pa yis/_/'jig rten pha rol don mi 'gyur/_/'jig rten pha rol don gnyer pa'i/_/skyes bu de yi 'bras

[\[283b.3\]]'tshal te/_dga' chen khyod bdag ston pa bas/_/slob dpon khyod ni bdag la dgongs/_/byang chub chen po'i tshul bstan pa/_/mgon po chen po bdag 'tshal lo/_/dam tshig de nyid bdag la stsol/_/byang chub sems ni bdag la stsol/_/sangs rgyas chos

[\[283b.5\]]gzengs bstod par bya ste/_khyod ni tshul chen snod yin te/_/bu tshur theg pa chen po yi/_/gsang sngags spyod tshul cho ga ni/_/khyod la yang dag de bstan bya/_/rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gang 'das dang /_/de bzhin gang dag ma byon dang /_/da ltar byung ba'i mgon po

[\[304a.6\]]ni sum 'bum du/_/phal cher ngas ni bstan pa dag_/lus can sdig dang bral ba yi/_/sngags pa rnam par dag pa las/_/bzlas pa'i grangs ni bstan pa ste/_/gzhan du bya ba ma yin no/_/zhes gsungs pas so/_/sngags pa 'bring gi dbang du byas te/_yang na drug ces bya 
[\[304a.7\]]ba smos te/_'bum phrag ces pa tshig lhag ma'o/_/sngags pa sman pa dag gis ni/_/dus yun ring por bzlas pa bya dgos te/_'di skad du/_lo grangs bcu gnyis dag bsnyen pas/_/las rnams thams cad nus par 'gyur/_/zhes gsungs la/_yang gzhan las/_
[\[304b\]]
[\[304b.1\]]nyes pa chen po byed pa yang /_/lo bcu gnyis kyis 'grub par 'gyur/_/zhes gsungs pas so/_/yang /_bye brag sum 'bum bzhi khri rtsod dus sbyar/_/zhes gsungs pas/_de'i dbang du byas te/_dngos grub mtshan ma byung bar du/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_'di'i tshad ni 'di 
[\[304b.2\]]las gzhan ci yang bya bar mi nus pas so/_/de la dngos grub kyi mtshan ma mngon sum pa ni/_sngar rgyas par bstan par zad do/_/rmi lam gyi mtshan ma phra ba ni/_/ji skad du 'phags pa dpung [{]bzangs kyis [,]bzang gis [}]zhus pa las/_de ltar bzlog pa'i gsang sngags nyer 'gyur na/_/
[\[304b.3\]]mtshan mo nyal na rmi lam 'di dag mthong /_/khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]ljon shing bram ze seng ge rta/_/ri bo shing rta sgra ldan sprin dang ni/_/stag dang bse dang glang chen khyu mchog la/_/rmi lam na ni dga' bas zhon pa dang /_/me tog phreng ba dri dang gos dang ni/_/sha chang nya dang 'bras bu glegs bam 
[\[304b.4\]]dang /_/lha yi gzugs dang ut+pal dmar po dang /_/shing rta gang la'am ba bzhon be'u phrug can/_/gdugs dkar bsil yab lham dang ral gyi dang /_/rnga yab gser yu can dang phreng ba dang /_/nor bu mu tig nya phyis dung dang dngul/_/bud med gzugs bzang ba dang bu mo dang /_/

[\[305a.1\]]zla ba gcig dang zla phyed nyin zhag dang /_/skad cig gis kyang phyi phyir nye bar 'gyur/_/zhes ji skad gsungs pa'o/_/gzhan yang nad med pa la sogs pa'i mtshan mas mtshan par bya'o/_/de lta bas na bsnyen pa phal pa byas kyang /_dngos grub chen po 'dod na ni/_/

[\[305b\]]
[\[305b.1\]]la sogs pa gang zhig don du gnyer ba de mnyes par byas te/_des bdag dbang bskur la gtor ma rgya cher byin te/_de nas dpa' bo bzhi dang sgrub pa'i grogs mchog gam/_yang na sgrub pa'i grogs mchog 'ba' zhig dang sgrub pa la 'jug par bya ste/_dkyil 'khor gyi bdag por

[\[308b.1\]]ba'i tshul du ma lus par 'dir bya ste/_cung zad du na 'chad par byed pa ltar ro/_/legs par brgyan te rdo rje lcags kyu'i sngags dang phyag rgyas ro'i rnam par shes pa bkug la/_de nas sta gon la gnas pa byas nas dbang bskur bar bya'o/_/de la rdo rje lcags kyu'i sngags dang phyag rgya

[\[308b.3\]]bcu dag la gnas pa dang /_/sdang bar sems pa gzhan dag kyang /_/thams cad rab tu 'gugs par byed/_/ces bya ba ni 'di'i mthu bstan pa ste/_'di'i sngags ni/_na maHsa man+ta bu d+d+hA nAM a/_sarba tra a pra ti ha ta ta thA ga ta aM ku sha bo d+hi tsa ra ya pa ri pU ri ka swA hA/_zhes pa ste
